31. Teletypewriter Printout

   a. A printout of the fire mission, the firing point list, or the target list may be obtained if the
teletypewriter is connected to the computer. Matrix positions A-7, B-7, and C-7 are used, re-
spectively.

                          ROCKET REFERENCE DATA

      1. The rocket programs compute weight information based upon the type rocket, warhead,

and launcher being used; therefore, manual computations are unnecessary. Table 4 shows the
matrix input function for weight data applicable to the rocket types.

E-3 (PROP WT CORR)        MGR-1A, -1B, and -3A  Weight stenciled on motor.
E-4 (FIN WT)              MGR-1B and 3A         166 pounds or 172 pounds according to fin type.
E-5 (PROP WT)             MGR-1A only           Weight stenciled on motor. Use only when E-1 is not
